## 4. Preflight method

Each row was checked for stable ID, page and canonical location, current state/wording, bounded candidate or hold instruction, stable source IDs or explicit U3 no-source boundary, locator or reproducible blocker, content nature, evidence status, planning alignment, readiness/disposition, and human pending fields. Verdict precedence: structural absence is `FAIL`; evidence/readiness or U3 owner dependency is `BLOCKED`; otherwise a normalized row is `PASS` for human-review packaging only.

All 846 rows below retain: `review_decision=pending_review`, `reviewer_agent=content-independent-review-agent`, `reviewer_identity=not_assigned`, `reviewer_role=not_assigned`, `reviewed_at=not_assigned`, and `implementation_gate=closed`.

## 6. Package conclusions

- **FAN-119 / FAN-131:** 149 rows, 149 unique IDs, PASS 95 / BLOCKED 54. All former row-materialization defects are repaired. Both D3 pages remain incomplete because blocker-only rows still lack lawful substantive closure.
- **FAN-120 / FAN-132:** 50 rows, 50 unique IDs, PASS 50 / BLOCKED 0. The four former blockers now have bounded proposals and lawful locators or attributed secondary support; two remain `partially_supported`, but all four are explicitly human-reviewable with fail-closed hold alternatives.
- **FAN-121:** 479 rows, 479 unique IDs, PASS 193 / BLOCKED 286. All 43 pages remain incomplete; blocker-only omission rows cannot be approved as public wording.
- **FAN-122 / FAN-134:** 152 rows, 152 unique IDs, PASS 0 / BLOCKED 152. FAN-134 closes disposition traceability (`revise=68`, `hold=84`) but does not close evidence/readiness; all remain blocker-only.
- **FAN-133 U3:** 16 rows, 16 unique status-change IDs, all exactly aligned to the 16 FAN-121 hide candidates. They contain state, derived-surface, rollback, and human pending slots, but explicit owner authorization is still absent.

## 7. Item-verdict creation boundary

1. The **338 approvable claim rows** can be sharded into at least two Paperclip `request_item_verdicts` interactions because the API allows at most 200 items per interaction. Use a frozen target revision, `allowBulkApprove=false`, and require reasons for reject/defer.
2. The **492 blocker-only claim rows must not be mixed into an approve-capable interaction**. Paperclip requires every item-verdict interaction to enable both `approve` and `reject` and has no per-item verdict restriction; mixing blocker-only rows would create an unsafe approval affordance. Keep them in the frozen report/backlog until evidence/readiness closes, or use a non-approval review mechanism.
3. The **16 U3 rows require a separate owner-only interaction**. An academic/content reviewer PASS cannot authorize archive/hide, routing, indexing, sitemap, advertising, publication, or deployment.
4. Before creating interactions, persist this frozen R3 package as a Paperclip issue document and bind each interaction to its latest revision ID. A local path alone is not a stable interaction target.
5. Human identity, role, real review date, verdict, rationale, and approved wording/state remain absent by design. These are the intended outputs of later human/owner interactions, not defects to fill by an Agent.
